#### 重要提示

公司董事会及董事、高级管理人员保证本报告及摘要所载资料不存在任何虚假记载、误导性陈述或者重大遗漏，并对其内容的真实性、准确性和完整性承担个别及连带责任。

公司年度财务报告已经中兴华会计师事务所（特殊普通合伙）根据财政部颁布的《企业会计准则（2006）》审计，并出具了标准无保留意见的审计报告。

本年度报告及摘要中比较列报的以前年度财务数据已经中兴华会计师事务所（特殊普通合伙）根据财政部颁布的《企业会计准则（2006）》进行审计调整。

本年度报告中的财务数据为合并报表经审计后的数据，资本相关数据及主要经营指标按照相关监管要求合并报表口径计算得出。

公司董事长李敏实、行长谭乐清以及财务负责人段霞，保证年度报告中财务报告的真实、完整。

莱商银行股份有限公司董事会

# Statement of Fiduciary Responsibility

The Board of Directors, the directors and senior management of the Company undertake that there are no misrepresentation, misleading statement, or material omission in the Report and its summary, and accept joint and several liability for the authenticity, accuracy and completeness of the contents of the Report.

The annual financial report of the Company has been audited by Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP (special general partnership) according to the Accounting Standards for Business Enterprises (2006) promulgated by the Ministry of Finance and a standard unqualified auditor's report has been issued.

The financial data of previous years listed in this annual report and in its summary for comparison have been audited and adjusted by Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP (special general partnership) according to the Accounting Standards for Business Enterprises (2006) promulgated by the Ministry of Finance.

The financial data listed in this annual report from consolidated statements being audited and the data related to capital and main business indicators are calculated by consolidating statement specifications according to supervision requirement.

Chairman Li Minshi, President Tan Leqing and Finance Director Duan Xia confirm the authenticity and completeness of the financial statements in the Annual Report.

Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.

## 七、公司各类风险和风险管理情况

董事会及其专门委员会、高级管理层及其专业委员会、风险管理部门和相关业务部门、风险报告员等构成公司风险管理的组织架构。公司董事会承担风险管理的最终责任。董事会下设风险管理委员会，负责公司风险管理政策的制定。高级管理层负责风险管理政策的具体实施，设立了信贷审查委员会、资产负债管理委员会等专业委员会，负责条线风险管理，并确立了在总分行设立合规风险部、在部室和支行设立风险报告员的风险管理模式。公司坚持全面风险管理理念，持续推行“全员控风险”的风险管控文化，制定了一系列风险管理制度和操作规程，针对各类风险制定了相应的应对措施，并实施了行之有效的风险管理和控制活动。今年以来，公司启动了全面风险管理体系建设，逐步搭建起符合监管要求的全面风险管理体系。公司面临的主要风险有信用风险、操作风险、流动性风险、市场风险、声誉风险、合规风险等，其中又以信用风险、操作风险、流动性风险为主。

## (一) 各类主要风险的计量方法

根据《商业银行资本管理办法》，公司目前采用权重法对信用风险加权资产进行计量，随着信用风险管理系统的建设和应用，公司将逐步实现以内部评级法计量信用风险。公司采用标准法计量市场风险加权资产，市场风险的计量覆盖了交易账户中的利率风险以及全部的汇率风险。公司目前采取基本指标法计量操作风险加权资产，公司将加强对操作风险管理工具的研究，逐步实现以标准法计量操作风险。

## （二）信用风险管理

信用风险管理的目标是确保将公司的信用风险水平控制在董事会和高级管理层确定的指标之内。2014年，公司通过合理配置信贷资源，继续推行法人客户统一授信管理，积极清收化解不良贷款，保证了公司信贷业务平稳健康发展。一是规范操作流程，完善操作指南。按照“机构扁平化、业务垂直化”的要求，公司积极推进管理架构和业务流程再造，针对不同的信贷业务种类，细化职责，对各层级、各环节制定格式化的操作标准，从申请、调查、审批、发放、贷后管理和清收等各环节，明确每个步骤操作指南，重要风险点控制要求，容易失误问题警示，对违规行为作出条款定义，健全违规、失职责任追究制度。二是加强信贷人员业务培训。年度内开展了4次信贷业务技能培训和考核工作，有效提升一线信贷员独立调查、分析能力，实现风险信息客观、完整、准确向中后台的传递，有效提升贷前调查的全面性、客观性。三是密切关注产业结构调整的影响。公司建立了过剩产能、政府融资平台、钢贸等重点领域风险监测台账，建立风险信息收集、分析和发布机制，预判风险趋势，提示新暴露风险，按月监测并及时报送监管部门，同时严格落实集中度、全口径等管理要求，陈旧控新，进一步降低贷款集中度。四是坚持服务中小企业的市场定位不动摇，继续推行法人客户统一授信工作。五是加强信贷“三查”工作，严控不良贷款反弹。密切关注信贷客户经营变化，寻求多元化方式处置不良贷款，对不良贷款实施责任认定和追究，落实“尽职免责”制度。

## (三) 市场风险管理

市场风险管理的目标是通过将市场风险控制在可以承受的合理范围内，实现经风险调整的收益最大化。公司面临的市场风险主要集中在贷款利率、外汇交易、债券投资等业务。针对上述风险，公司主要采取以下措施：一是面对利率市场化的发展压力，公司密切关注国家宏观调控政策、产业结构的调整，正确分析和把握利率市场化对传统业务结构和盈利模式的影响，积极调整业务结构，强化利率风险管理，加快经营战略转型，在市场营销、内部控制、风险管理、考核激励等方面及时做出调整，不断提高市场风险管理能力。二是强化资产负债结构管理，维持利差稳定。根据利率风险偏好，公司合理优化资产负债期限结构，有效控制利率敏感性缺口，并将投资组合与资产负债结构调整紧密配合，实现了净利息收益的稳定增长。三是为有效控制市场风险，公司内审部组织了货币市场业务审计，对公司货币市场业务的开展、岗位设置、账户设立的合规性、内部控制及风险管理状况进行了现场审计，并认真进行了整改。四是对市场风险实行限额管理，建立并实行了对各类和各级限额的内部审批程序和操作规程，能够根据业务性质、规模、复杂程度和风险承受能力设定定期审查并及时进行限额更新。五是加强风险管理人员的培训、考核和对市场的调研，提升员工市场风险识别与防控能力，提高市场风险管理水平，保证市场风险管理工作的顺利开展。

## (四)操作风险管理

操作风险管理的目标是建立与公司的业务性质、规模和复杂程度相适应的操作风险管理体系，实现公司稳健运营，提升核心竞争力。公司持续开展思想道德教育，梳理完善内控制度，加强监督检查工作力度，取得了良好的效果。一是为规范业务操作流程，增强全员合规经营意识，公司组织编写了合规手册，覆盖计划财务、公司银行、个人银行、国际业务、金融市场等六个业务条线的产品，进一步规范了操作步骤，有效防范了业务操作风险。二是实行风险排查动态管理，严防操作风险和道德风险，重点关注员工日常行为动态变化，实施员工行为动态排摸，并建立起了完整的员工电子管理档案。三是开展全员教育培训。公司坚持以岗位需求为导向，为员工量身订制中长期培训规划，强化员工职业道德及业务素质教育，形成了全方位多层次的培训体系。今年以来陆续开展了理财规划师、保险和证券资格认证、小微企业信贷业务、诉讼案件管理、反洗钱工作和合规岗位等培训活动，员工综合素质显著提升。四是加强各项制度的贯彻落实，强化内部考核和处罚，严格执行分级授权制度，业务运营质量显著提升。五是积极开展监督检查，及时整改管理漏洞。公司持续开展检查与自查活动，对柜员尾箱、信贷业务、支付结算、代理国库、反洗钱、外汇业务、安全保卫、消防等各方面都进行了彻底检查，提高了业务水平和服务质量，消除了风险隐患。

## (五)流动性风险管理

流动性风险管理的目标是通过建立适时、合理、有效的流动性风险管理机制，实现对流动性风险的识别、计量、监测和控制，将流动性风险控制在可以承受的范围之内，以推动公司的持续、稳健运行。一是资产负债期限较为匹配，公司中长期贷款余额占比为4.04%，流动性较为充足。二是2014年建设了头寸管理系统和流动性风险管理系统，有利于公司在监控未来现金流、完善流动性监测、预警机制方面加强信息管理，确保公司保持充足的流动性。三是持续改善流动性管理，完善应急预案，公司先后开展4次流动性压力测试，提高前瞻性的风险防范。四是保持较强的短期资金获取能力。在流动性资金不足时公司能够通过同业拆入、再贴现、再贷款、债券回购和同业授信等方式以合理价格获取资金。继续加强与浦发银行的合作，与浦发银行签订了37.5亿元的信用拆借额度协议。

## (六) 合规风险管理

合规风险管理的目标是通过建立健全合规风险管理框架，实现对合规风险的有效识别和管理，促进全面风险管理体系建设，确保依法合规经营。公司采取了多种措施保证合规风险管理目标的实现。一是开办的所有业务均以法律法规和监管制度为准绳，并根据监管要求的变化及时更新内控制度和操作规程，严格做到合规经营。二是年内开展了全面风险管理状况评估，制定了全面风险管理架构体系的详细规划，公司将参照实施规划报告和路线图，进一步梳理完善全面风险管理架构。三是在公司合规风险部设立合规审核岗，对所有正式行文、内控制度及信贷业务进行合规性审核。四是不定期

组织多种形式的法律知识培训和讲座，提升了员工的法律合规意识。

## 八、内部控制制度的完整性、合理性与有效性

公司继续以防范风险、审慎经营为出发点，本着全面、审慎、有效、独立的原则不断加强内部控制，全面贯彻执行各类内部控制法规，实现了各项业务的快速发展和稳健运行。

一是逐步健全完善的公司法人治理结构。股东大会作为权力机构，董事会作为决策机构，监事会作为监督机构，高级管理层作为日常经营管理机构，公司形成了分责、分权、制衡的有效机制。逐步加强总行对全系统、上级行对下级行的集中管理与监督，加强各专业部门的系统管理职能，强化系统风险控制；在全行范围内适度集中资源，提高经营层次，实现集约化经营和整体效益最大化。

二是健全内部监督机制。促进内部控制建设，建立科学有效、防患于未然的监督防范体系，把加强内控作为防范银行风险的关键环节。健全独立有效的内部审计制度，充分发挥内部审计职能，加强监督检查，强化制度落实。全年持续开展各类全面和专项审计42次，范围涉及会计、财务、授信、自助设备、货币市场业务等各类重点业务、重点环节、重点岗位和人员，全面监督内控制度的落实情况及各项业务的经营状况。通过对审计发现内部控制存在的问题及时予以纠正，找出内控制度中的缺失和漏洞予以补充，有效提升制度执行力，促进内部控制进一步完善。

三是梳理规章制度，进一步完善制度体系。公司针对各项业务形成了更为全面、规范、系统的管理制度和操作规程，使内部控制渗透到各项业务过程和各个操作环节、覆盖所有部门和岗位，确保公司能够保持统一的业务标准和规范要求。

四是建立健全内控制度评价机制。对内部控制制度的建设、执行情况，均由专门人员根据国家法律规定、业务发展状况和市场环境变化等定期进行审计评价，实现制度管理的常态化、持续化和规范化，有效保障内控制度的完整性、合规性和可操作性。

五是加强合规管理，积极开展合规文化建设。公司继续大力开展合规理念教育，积极倡导“合规人人有责”，把规章制度变成员工日常工作中的一种自觉行为规范，深化全员合规人人有责、合规创造价值等合规理念，倡导每位员工主动发现和暴露合规风险隐患或问题，形成主动遵守各项规章制度的良好氛围，有效提升内部控制效力，从源头上防范风险事件的发生。

## VII. Risks of the Company and risk management

The organizational structure for risk management of the Company consists of the Board of Directors and its special committee, Senior Management and its special committee, the risk management department and the related business department, and risk reporters etc. The Board of Directors is ultimately responsible for risk management. Under the Board of Directors is a Risk Management Committee, which is responsible for formulating risk management policy of the Company. The senior management is responsible for the specific implementation of risk management policies, under which special committees such as the Credit Review Committee and the Assets-Liabilities Management Committee have been set up, which is responsible for risk management of business lines; the senior management also establishes a risk management mode: compliance risk departments are set up in the head office and the branches and risk reporters are appointed for departments and sub-branches. Sticking to the idea of comprehensive risk management, the Company implements risk management concept of "all staff involved in risk control" and formulates a series of risk management system and operation instructions. The Company has prepared solutions to address each kind of risk and has implemented effective risk management and control activities. The Company has launched comprehensive risk management system construction since this year and has set up a comprehensive risk management system which conforms to supervision.

requirement. Major risks the Company confronts include credit risk, operational risk, liquidity risk, market risk, reputation risk and compliance risk etc., among which, credit risk, operational risk and liquidity risk are the main risks.

#### (i) Measurement method for each kind of main risk

The Company adopts method of weighting to measure credit risk-weighted asset according to Administrative Measures for the Capital of Commercial Banks. With construction and application of credit risk management system, the company will adopt internal rating-based approach to measure credit risks gradually. The Company adopts standardized approach to measure market risk-weighted asset. Market risk measurement covers interest rate risk in transaction account and all exchange rate risks. The Company adopts basic indicator approach to measure operational risk-weighted asset at present. The company will strength research of operational risk management tools and measure operational risks by standardized approach gradually.

#### (ii) Management of credit risk

Management of credit risk aims to control credit risk level of the Company within the indicator specified by Board of Directors and Senior Management. The Company continued to carry out unified credit management of corporate clients through reasonable allocation of credit resources, cleaned and resolved bad loans actively and ensured stable and sound development of credit operations of the Company in 2014. Firstly, standardize operation process and complete operation instruction. The Company implemented management architecture and business process reengineering according to the requirements of "flattened organization and vertical business". It specified formative operation standard in each layer and link targeting different types of credit businesses by refining responsibilities. It determined operation instruction, principle risk point control requirement and easily fault problems warning for each process such as application, investigation, approval, issue, management after loan and clean-up. It made clause definition for illegal behavior and completed accountability system for irregulations and derelication. Secondly, strengthen credit personnel's business training. The Company carried out credit operations skill training and assessment work for 4 times within the year, which improved first-line credit clerks' ability of independent investigation and analysis effectively. Risk information can be sent to the background objectively, completely and accurately, which improved comprehensiveness and objectivity of pre-loan investigation. Thirdly, pay close attention to the influence of industrial restructuring. The Company established risk monitoring standing books for key fields such as excess production capacity, government financing vehicles and steel trade etc. It established risk information collection, analysis and issuing mechanism to predict and judge risk tendency, prompt newly-exposed risks, monitor them monthly and submit them to supervision department promptly. At the same time, it implemented management requirements such as concentration ratio and full specification strictly, decreased old requirement and controlled new one to reduce loan concentration ratio further. Fourthly, stick to the market positioning to serve middle and small-sized enterprises and carry out unified credit management of corporate clients continuously. Fifth, strengthen "3-inspection" for credit and control bad loan rebound strictly. The Company paid close attention to credit clients' operation changes, sought diversification method to handle bad loans, implemented bad loan accountability system as well as the system of "liability exemption by fulfill the duty".

#### (iii) Management of market risk

Management of market risk aims to realize revenue maximization after risk adjustment by controlling market risk within bearable and reasonable scope. Market risk confronted by the Company are mainly in loan interest rate, foreign exchange transaction and bond investment. The Company adopted the following measures to handle above risks: Firstly, faced with the development pressure of interest rate liberalization, the Company paid close attention to national macro-control policy and readjustment of industrial structure, analyzed and mastered influences of interest rate liberalization on traditional business structure and profit model, adjusted business structure actively, reinforced interest rate risk management,

accelerated business strategy transformation, adjusted marketing, internal control, risk management and assessment and motivation and promoted market risk management capacity. Secondly, strengthen assets and liabilities structure management and maintain stability of interest margin. The Company optimized assets and liabilities term structure reasonably according to interest rate risk preference and controlled interest rate sensitivity gap effectively, combined investment portfolio and structural adjustment of assets and liabilities closely to realize stable increase of net interest income. Thirdly, in order to control market risks effectively, Internal Audit Department of the Company organized monetary market business audit. They audited monetary market business launch, post setting, compliance of account setting, and internal control and risk management condition on site and rectified and reformed the above carefully. Fourthly, in order to implement quota management for market risks, the Company established and implemented internal approval process and operation instruction with all kinds and all levels of quotas. The Company could set periodic audit and update quota promptly according to business property, scale, complexity and risk tolerance. Fifthly, strengthen risk management personnel's training and assessment and market research, improve staff's market risk identification and prevention and control capacity, improve market risk management level and ensure smooth and success of market risk management.

##### (iv) Management of operational risk

Management of operational risk aims to establish operational risk management system which is applicable to business property, scale and complexity of the Company, realize stable operation and improve core competitiveness. The Company has carried out ideological and moral education, completed internal control system and strengthened supervision and inspection, which led to a good effect. Firstly, the Company prepared compliance manual, which covered products of 6 business lines such as planning and finance, corporate banking, personal banking, international business and financial market etc. in order to standardize business operation process and strengthen personnel's compliance operation awareness. This standardized operation process further and prevented business operation risks effectively. Secondly, the Company implemented risk troubleshooting dynamic management, prevented operational risk and moral risk, emphasized on dynamic change of staff's daily behavior, modeled staff's behavior dynamically and established complete electronic management file for staff. Thirdly, the Company carried out entire personnel education training. The Company stuck to post requirement orientation, customized medium and long term training plan for staff and strengthened staff professional ethics and business quality education to form a comprehensive and multi-layered training system. It has carried out many training activities such as financial planner, insurance and securities qualification, small and micro enterprise credit business, lawsuit management, anti-money laundering, compliance post, etc. Staff's comprehensive quality increased obviously. Fourthly, strengthen implementation of each system, reinforce internal assessment and punishment, implement hierarchical authorization system strictly and business operation quality has been promoted remarkably. Fifthly, carry out supervision and inspection actively to correct and manage loopholes. The Company carried out inspection and self-inspection activities continuously and inspected all aspects such as teller cash-box, credit business, payment settlement, fiscal agent, anti-money laundering, foreign exchange transaction, security and fire control, which promoted business level and service quality and eliminated hidden danger of risks.

#### (V) Management of liquidity risk

Management of liquidity risk aims to identify, measure, monitor and control liquidity risks and control liquidity risks within an acceptable range so as to advance sustainable and stable operation of the Company through establishing appropriate, reasonable and effective liquidity risk management system. Firstly, assets and liabilities were matching, medium-term and long-term loan balance of the Company accounted for 4.04% and its liquidity was sufficient. Secondly, position management system and liquidity risk management system were established in 2014, which were convenient for the Company to strengthen information management and keep enough liquidity in terms of monitoring future cash flow, completing liquidity monitoring and early-warning mechanism. Thirdly, the Company improved liquidity management continuously and completed contingency plan. It has carried out liquidity pressure tests for 4 times to promote prospective risk prevention. Fourthly, keep strong ability to gain short-term funds.

Company could gain funds with reasonable price through inter-bank borrowing, rediscount, re-loan, bond repurchase and inter-bank credit granting when liquid capital was insufficient. The Company strengthened cooperation with Shanghai Pudong Development Bank and signed 3.75 billion yuan credit lending limit agreement with Shanghai Pudong Development Bank.

##### (VI) Management of compliance risk

Management of compliance risk aims to recognize and manage compliance risk effectively, promote overall risk management system construction and ensure legal and compliance operation through establishing and improving compliance risk management framework. The Company adopted many measures to realize compliance risk management goals. Firstly, all businesses it conducted conformed to laws and regulations and regulatory system. Internal control system and operation instruction were updated promptly according to changes of supervision requirement. Operation must conform to regulations. Secondly, total risk management condition assessment was conducted in the year and detailed planning of total risk management architecture system was made. The Company will complete total risk management architecture further by reference to implementation of the planning report and roadmap. Thirdly, compliance audit post was set in Compliance Risk Department of the Company, which conducted compliance audit of all official documents, internal control system and credit operations. Fourthly, the Company organized various legal knowledge training and lecture irregularly to promote staff's legal compliance awareness.

## VIII. Completeness, reasonableness and effectiveness of the internal control system

The Company continuously strengthened internal control, comprehensively implemented various internal control regulations and achieved rapid development and sound operation of all businesses with comprehensive, prudent, effective and independent principle on the basis of risk prevention and prudent operation.

Firstly, the corporate governance structure was gradually strengthened and perfected. The Company, with the shareholders' general meeting as its organ of authority, Board of Directors as the decision-making body, Board of Supervisors as the supervisory body and Senior Management as daily operation management organization, has formed an effective mechanism of separated responsibilities, separated powers, checks and balances, gradually strengthening centralized management and supervision of Head Office to the total system and superior to inferior, system administration to the specialized department and system risk control, moderately centralizing the resource within the whole bank, improving management level and finally realizing intensive management and maximum overall benefits.

Secondly, the internal supervision mechanism was improved. Internal control and construction was enhanced to establish a scientific and efficient, precautious supervision and prevention system. The intensification of internal control was regarded as a key point in preventing bank risks. The independent and effective internal audit system was established to bring the function of internal audit into full play and strengthen supervision and inspection and intensify system implementation. All kinds of comprehensive and specific audits were carried out for 42 times throughout the year, involving accounting, finance, credit granting, self-service equipment, money market operations and other key business, key links, key posts and personnel, in order to give a general supervision of the implementation of internal control system and the operation of various businesses. By correcting immediately the problems existing in internal control in the process of auditing, deficiencies and loopholes of internal control system can be found and supplemented, which effectively improved system execution and further perfected the internal system.

Thirdly, rules and regulations were combined to further improve the institutional system. The Company has formed a more comprehensive, standard and systematic management system and operation instructions for all businesses, by which internal control can spread in all operation steps and cover all department and posts, maintaining uniform business standards and requirements.

Fourthly, evaluation mechanism for internal control system was established and improved.

Establishment and execution of internal control system were audited and evaluated by professionals in accordance with national legal provisions, business development condition, and change of market environment to achieve normalization, sustainability and standardization of system management and effectively guarantee completeness, compliance and operability of internal control system.

Fifthly, compliance management was strengthened and compliance cultural construction was carried out actively. The Company continued to carry out compliance concept education and advocated "everyone is responsible for compliance", which turned the rules and regulations into a natural behavior of employees in their daily work and deepened compliance idea of everyone being responsible for compliance and compliance creating value of employees. The Company also advocated that employees could find and expose hidden danger of risks or problems of compliance by themselves and formed a good atmosphere that they complied with various rules and regulations initially. The system learning activity enhanced the internal control effectiveness and prevented the occurrence of risk events from the source.

## （五）董事、监事、高级管理人员薪酬情况

### 1. 薪酬管理架构及决策程序

公司理顺了董事会、监事会、高管层等治理主体在薪酬管理中的职责、重点和任务，建立了科学的薪酬管理架构，明确规定董事、监事的报酬由股东大会决定，董事会决定高级管理人员的报酬。董事会下设提名与薪酬考核委员会，根据公司经营效益情况研究拟订董事会、监事会、高级管理层成员和员工的薪酬水平及构成，包括养老和各种补偿性支付等，提出年度薪酬方案和意见并报董事会审议通过后执行。

### 2. 年度薪酬情况

2014年，公司薪酬总量为16113万元，包括：固定薪酬、可变薪酬和福利性收入。

3. 薪酬与业绩衡量、风险调整的标准

公司绩效工资按照《2014年分支机构考核办法》进行考核调整后兑现。

4. 薪酬制度、延期支付和非现金薪酬情况

公司自2010年实施新的薪酬制度——等级行员制，按照“以责定岗、岗位设置精简、专业化”的原则，根据员工岗位职责及标准进行人岗匹配，实行了以岗定薪、按月拨付的固定薪酬。

公司可变薪酬中包含了绩效薪酬和中长期激励约束基金。

自2013年起，公司绩效薪酬发放实行延期支付制度，延期支付时间为3年。其中，高管绩效薪酬的50%实行延期支付，按照兑付50%之后第一年15%，第二年15%，第三年20%的比例

分三年兑付。

中长期激励约束基金也实行延期支付，按照当年30%，第二年30%，第三年40%的比例分三期兑付。

##### 公司无非现金薪酬情况。

股东单位出任的董事、监事和独立董事、外部监事2014年度共领取薪酬40万元。

6. 年度薪酬方案制定及考核完成情况

公司年度薪酬方案根据人员规模、业务发展情况等制定，2014年度，公司各项经营指标良好，薪酬情况合理的反应了公司业绩及风险管理情况。

7.超出原定薪酬方案的例外情况

报告期内，公司无超出原定薪酬的例外情况。

## （六）报告期内离任的董事、监事、高级管理人员姓名及离任原因

报告期内，因工作原因，独立董事王进诚辞职。

## 二、员工情况

报告期末，公司在职员工人数为1653人，较上年增加302人；员工中大学以上学历1623人，占比98.19%。

##### (v) Remunerations of directors, supervisors and senior management

1. Remunerations management structure and decision-making procedure

The Company has rationalized the responsibilities, key points and tasks of governance bodies such as the Board of Directors, Board of Supervisors and senior management in remunerations management. A scientific remuneration management structure has been established, specifying that the remunerations of directors and supervisors is decided by the Shareholders' General Meeting, and the remunerations of senior management is decided by the Board of Directors. Under the Board of Directors is a Nomination and remunerations Assessment Committee, subject to the operational efficiency of the Company, which prepares the remunerations level and remunerations composition of the Board of Directors, Board of Supervisors, members of senior management and employees, including pension and various compensatory payment etc., and puts forward the annual remunerations plan and opinion, to be implemented after being reviewed and approved by the Board of Directors.

2. The total amount and structure distribution of remunerations in the year

In 2014, the total amount of remunerations of the Company was RMB 161.13 million. The remunerations structure are consist of fixed remunerations, variable remunerations and welfare income.

3. Remunerations and the standard of performance measurement and risk adjustment

The Company’s performance salary is assessed and paid according to the 2014 Measures on Assessment of Branches. The Company has established the risk fund system and prepared the Management Measures on Risk Fund, which sets out that all employees from Chairman to ordinary employees shall pay risk fund. The risk fund shall be return in full or pro rata according to auditing results when any employee leaves the Company.

4. Salary system, deferred payment and non-cash compensation

The Company has implemented new salary system (grading system) since 2010. According to the principle of “responsibility-based position, simple position setting, specialization”, Person-post matching is conducted based on job responsibilities of employees and standards and fixed salary confirmed by post and paid monthly has been implemented.

Variable salary of the Company includes performance salary and medium and long-term inventive and restrictive fund.

The issuing of performance salary of the Company has been provided with deferred payment system since 2013, with deferred payment time of 3 years. 50% of performance salary of senior management is provided with deferred payment and payment shall be made in three years after paying 50%, with 15% for the first year, 15% for the second year and 20% for the third year.

Medium and long-term inventive and restrictive fund is provided with deferred payment and payment

shall be made in three years, with 30% for current year, 30% for the second year and 40% for the third year.

There is no non-cash remuneration in the Company.

5. The annual remuneration of directors, supervisors and senior management are as follows:

6. Preparation of annual remuneration plan and completion of assessment

The Company’s annual remuneration plan is prepared according to the size of staff and business development, etc. In 2014, various operational indicators of the Company were good, and remuneration reasonably reflected the performance and risk management of the company.

There were no exceptions beyond the original remuneration plan in the reporting period of the Company.

## (vi) Names of directors, supervisors and senior management who resigned during the reporting period and reasons for resignation

During the reporting period, independent Wang Jincheng resigned due to work arrangements.

## II. Employee profile

At the end of the reporting period, there were 1,653 employees in the Company, increasing by 302 from the previous year; among whom employees with a bachelor's degree or above amounted to 1,623, accounting for 98.19%.

### 第五章 公司治理结构

### 一、公司治理情况

报告期内，公司严格遵守《公司法》和《商业银行法》的有关规定，认真落实监管部门关于公司治理的有关规定，致力于进一步完善公司治理结构，增强公司治理机制的有效性，提高董事会决策的科学性。公司治理具体情况如下：

（一）关于股东与股东大会。公司按照《公司章程》和《股东大会议事规则》的要求召集、召开股东大会，积极听取股东的意见和建议，确保股东对公司重大事项的知情权、参与权和表决权。报告期内，公司召开了2013年度股东大会、2014年第一次临时股东大会，会议严格按照有关规定履行了相关法律程序，保证了股东参会并行使权利，并由律师出具了法律意见书。

（二）关于董事和董事会。公司现有董事12名，其中独立董事2名。董事会能够严格按照《公司章程》的规定，认真履行职责，在股东大会授权下进行决策，强化公司治理，推进战略步伐，促进稳健经营，维护了公司和全体股东的利益。报告期内公司共召开了4次董事会会议。

目前，公司董事会下设1个办公室和5个专门委员会，包括董事会办公室、关联交易控制委员会、风险管理委员会、战略发展委员会、审计委员会、提名与薪酬考核委员会，其中风险管理委员会下设声誉风险管理专业委员会和信息科技风险管理专业委员会。5个专门委员会具有明确的职权范围，监管公司不同方面的事务，并对董事会负责，为董事会决策提供了建设性建议，进一步提高了董事会决策的科学性。

（三）关于监事和监事会。公司现有监事7人，其中职工监事2人，外部监事2人。监事会按照《公司章程》的规定认真履行职责，对公司财务、风险管理、资产质量以及部分高级管理人员履职的合规性进行监督检查，定期及不定期地与外部审计机构和管理层进行情况交流，有针对性地开展专项检查，针对风险隐患和发现的问题，向董事会和管理层提出建议和改进意见，从而更加有效地发挥监事会的作用。

（四）关于信息披露与透明度。公司在《金融时报》上发布了2013年度报告，并制作了《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年度报告》（摘要）单行本，及时准确地进行信息披露，切实维护广大股东特别是中小投资者的利益。

### 二、独立董事及履行职责情况

报告期内，公司董事会现有独立董事2人，独立董事本着为全体股东负责的态度，履行诚信和勤勉义务，认真参加董事会及各专门委员会会议，积极发表意见，维护公司整体利益及中小股东的合法权益不受侵犯。

（一）独立董事参加会议情况。报告期内，独立董事应参加董事会会议8人次，亲自出席7人次，委托出席1人次，缺席0人次。

（二）独立董事对公司有关事项提出异议情况。报告期内，公司独立董事未对公司本年度董事会的议案提出异议。

## 三、外部监事工作情况

报告期内，公司监事会现有外部监事2人，外部监事本着为全体股东负责的态度，履行诚信和勤勉义务，认真参加监事会及各专门委员会会议，积极发表意见，维护公司整体利益及中小股东的合法权益不受侵犯。

（一）外部监事参加会议情况。报告期内，外部监事应参加监事会会议6人次，亲自出席6人次，委托出席0人次，缺席0人次。

（二）外部监事对公司有关事项提出异议情况。报告期内，公司外部监事未对公司本年度董事会、监事会的议案提出异议。

## 四、公司经营决策体系

公司最高权力机构是股东大会，通过董事会、监事会进行管理和监督。行长受聘于董事会，对公司日常经营管理全面负责。公司实行一级法人体制，各分支机构均为非独立核算单位，其经营管理活动根据公司授权进行，并对公司负责。

公司无控股股东。公司与大股东在业务、人员、资产、机构、财务五方面完全独立。公司具有独立完整的业务及自主经营能力，董事会、监事会和内部机构能够独立运作。

## 五、高级管理人员考评及激励机制、制度情况

报告期内，公司董事会进一步完善了对高级管理人员的绩效考核管理办法，建立了薪酬与公司效益和个人业绩相联系的激励约束机制；考核实行百分制，根据各高管人员的工作性质不同，考核项目有所侧重。对于非执行董事、非职工监事的考核主要以其出席会议次数及履职能力考核为主。制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会董事履职评价制度》，主要分为客观评价、自我评价和相互评价三个部分，根据各项指标具体内容的不同，给予不同的比重。2012年，公司制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会对高管人员履职评价办法》、《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对董事、监事、高管人员履职评价办法》、《关于追究董事、监事及高管人员责任的规定》。同时，公司监事会进一步量化考核标准，制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对高管人员履职评价办法实施细则》，明确了监事会对高级管理人员的履职评价，加大了履职问责力度。

# Chapter V Corporate Governance Structure

### 1. Corporate governance of the Company

During the reporting period, the Company has strictly observed the related provisions in the Company Law and the Law on Commercial Banks, conscientiously implemented the related regulations of the regulatory intuition on corporate governance, and been dedicated to further improving corporate governance structure, enhancing the effectiveness of the corporate governance mechanism and making the decision of the Board of Directors more scientific. The performance of the Company in its corporate governance is as follows:

##### (i) Shareholders and shareholders' general meeting.

The Company has convened the shareholders' general meeting in accordance with the requirements of the Articles of Association and the Rules of Procedures of Shareholders' Meeting and actively listened to shareholders' opinions and suggestions to ensure that all shareholders have the right to know, to participate and to vote to the major events of the Company. During the reporting period, the Company held its 2013 annual shareholders' general meeting, 2014 first extraordinary shareholders' general meeting. The meetings went through related legal procedure in strict accordance with related regulations to ensure that shareholders participate in the meetings and exercise their rights. Also, legal opinion was issued by a lawyer.

##### (ii) Directors and the Board of Directors.

Currently, the Company has 12 directors, including 2 independent directors. The Board of Directors is able to conscientiously perform their duties in strict accordance with the provisions in the Articles of Association, makes decisions with the authorization of the shareholders' general meeting, strengthens corporate governance, advances strategic steps and promotes sound operation which safeguarded the interests of the Company and all shareholders. During the reporting period, the Company held altogether 4 board meetings.

Currently, under the Board of Directors of the Company, there is 1 office and 5 special committees, including the office of the Board of Directors, related transaction control committee, risk management committee, strategic development committee, audit committee, and nomination and remuneration assessment committee. Under the risk management committee are the reputation risk management special committee and the information technology risk management special committee. The 5 special committees have specific scopes of power, are accountable to the Board of Directors. They provide constructive suggestions for the decision making of the Board of Directors in a timely manner to improve the science of the decisions make by the Board of Directors.

##### (iii) Supervisors and the Board of Supervisors.

The Company has 7 supervisors, including 2 employee supervisors and 2 external supervisors. The Board of Supervisors conscientiously performs its duties strictly in accordance with the provisions in the Articles of Association, carries out supervision and inspection to the compliance of the company finance, risk management, assets quality and the duty performance of the senior management, communicates with the external audit units and the management regularly and

irregularly, carries out targeted special inspection, and puts forward suggestions and improvement opinions to the Board of Directors and the management according to risks and problems discovered, so as to play the role of the Board of Supervisors more effectively.

##### (iv) Information disclosure and transparency.

The Company has published its 2013 Annual Report on Financial Times, and prepared a separate edition of 2013 Annual Report of Laishang Co., Ltd. (summary) to carry out information disclosure in a timely and accurate manner and safeguard the interests of shareholders, particularly medium and small shareholders.

## II. Independent directors and their performance of duties

During the reporting period, there were 2 independent directors on the Board of Directors. With an attitude of being responsible to all shareholders, independent directors performed the obligation of integrity and due diligence, earnestly participate in board meetings and meetings of various special committees and actively delivered their opinions to safeguard the collective interest of the Company and the legitimate rights and interest of medium and small shareholders.

##### (i) Independent directors' participation in meetings.

During the reporting period, the number of independent directors attending the board meetings shall be 8, with presence in 7 persons, entrusted attendance in 1 person, and none was absent.

##### (ii) Objections of the independent directors to related affairs of the Company.

During the reporting period, the independent directors of the Company did not object to any proposal of the Board of Directors of the year.

## III. Work of the External Supervisor

There are 2 external supervisors in the Board of Supervisors in the Company during the reporting period. The External Supervisor shall be responsible to all shareholders, perform duty of integrity and diligence, carefully participate in the meeting of the Board of Supervisors and all special committee meetings, positively give opinions and prevent collective interests of the Company and lawful rights and interests of medium and small shareholders from infringement.

##### (i) External Supervisors' participation in meeting.

During the reporting period, the number of External Supervisors attending the Board of Supervisors Meeting shall be 6, with presence in 6 persons, entrusted attendance in 0 person and none was absent.

##### (ii) Objection raised by the External Supervisor.

During the reporting period, the External Supervisors of the Company fail to raise objections to the proposals of the Board of Shareholders and Board of Supervisors of the Company for the current year.

## IV. Operation and decision-making system of the Company

The highest authority of the Company is the Shareholders' General Meeting, which is managed and supervised through the Board of Directors and the Board of Supervisors. The President, employed by the Board of Directors, is responsible for all daily operation and management of the Company. Only the head office of the Company is its legal person, and all branches and sub-branches are non-independent accounting units, whose operation and management activities shall be carried out with the authorization of the Company and who shall be responsible for the Company.

The Company has no controlling shareholders. The Company and its large shareholders are completely independent in terms of business, personnel, assets, organizations and finance. The Company has independent and complete business and independent operation ability, and the Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors and internal organizations can operate independently.

## V. Assessment and incentive mechanism and system of senior management

During the reporting period, the Board of Directors of the Company has further improved the method of performance assessment and management of its senior management, and established an incentive and constraint mechanism in which remunerations are associated with the performance of the Company and personal performance; the centesimal system has been carried out for assessment subject to the working nature of senior management and different management positions have different key points for assessment. The assessment of non-executive directors and non-employee supervisors has mainly focused on their attendance on meetings and ability to perform their duties. The Evaluation System of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Duty Performance of Directors on the Board of Directors, which includes such three parts as objective evaluation, self-evaluation and mutual evaluation, has been prepared. Different proportions are given according to different contents of various indexes. In 2012, the Measures of the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Assessment of Senior Management's Performance, the Measures of the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Assessment of Directors, Supervisors and Senior Management's Performance and the Provisions of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Accountability of Directors, Supervisors and Senior Management have been formulated. Meanwhile, the Board of Supervisors quantified the criteria of assessment, formulated the Implementing Rules of Duty Performance Assessment Method on Senior Management by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., specified the duty performance assessment on senior management by the Board of Supervisors and strengthened the accountability of duty performance.

## 第六章 股东大会情况

报告期内公司共召开了两次股东大会，具体情况如下：

2014年3月12日，在公司综合营业楼一楼礼堂召开了2013年度股东大会，出席会议的股东及股东代理人共85名，代表股份181932.992万股，占公司总股本的90.97%。大会审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会2013年度工作报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会2013年度工作报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年度审计报告》、《关于审议<莱商银行股份有限公司2013年度财务报告及利润分配方案>的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司业务转型创新发展规划》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2014年机构发展计划的报告》、《关于对<莱商银行股份有限公司2013年度报告>（摘要）进行信息披露的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年度内部人和股东关联交易执行情况的报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年度风险管理报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对董事2013年度履职评价报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对高级管理人员2013年度履职评价报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对监事2013年度履职评价报告》。

2014年10月9日，在公司综合营业楼六楼会议室召开了2014年第一次临时股东大会，出席会议的股东及股东代理人共82名，代表股份181993.552万股，占公司总股本的91%。大会审议通过了《关于更换会计师事务所的议案》、《关于修改公司章程的议案》、《关于购买济宁分行营业楼的议案》、《关于购买菏泽分行营业楼的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司三年资本规划（2014-2016）》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2014年1-6月内部人和股东关联交易执行情况的报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2014年1-6月风险管理报告》。

# Chapter VI Shareholders' General Meeting

Three shareholders general meeting of the Company have been held during the reporting period. Details are as follows:

On March 12, 2014, 2013 Annual General Meeting of Shareholders was held in the auditorium on the first floor of the comprehensive business building of the Company. 85 shareholders and their authorized agents attended the meeting, representing 1,819,329,920.00 shares, accounting for 90.97% of the total share capital of the Company. The meeting considered and passed 2013 Annual Work Report of the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2013 Annual Work Report of the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2013 Annual Audit Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Reviewing <2013 Annual Financial Report and Profit Distribution Plan of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.>, Business Transformation and Innovative Development Planning of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2014 Organizational Development Planning of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Information Disclosure in <2013 Annual Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.>(Abstract), Report on Executing Related Transactions by Insiders and Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. in 2013, 2013 Annual Risk Management Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2013 Annual Evaluation Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. Made by Board of Supervisors for the Resumption of Directors, 2013 Annual Evaluation Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. Made by Board of Supervisors for the Resumption of Senior Management, and 2013 Annual Evaluation Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. Made by Board of Supervisors for the Resumption of Supervisors.

On October 9, 2014, the First Extraordinary General Meeting in 2014 was held in the meeting room on the sixth floor of the comprehensive business building of the Company. 82 shareholders and their authorized agents attended the meeting, representing 1,819,935,520.00 shares, accounting for 91% of the total share capital of the Company. The meeting considered and passed Proposal on Replacing Accounting Firm, Proposal on Modifying Articles of Association, Proposal on Purchasing the Business Building of Jining Branch, Proposal on Purchasing the Business Building of Heze Branch, Three-Year (2014-2016) Capital Planning of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Report on Executing Related Transactions by Insiders and Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. from January, 2014 to June, 2014, and Risk Management Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. from January, 2014 to June, 2014.

## 一、2014年度主要经营管理情况

## （一）稳健经营科学发展，综合实力进一步提升

资产总额552.51亿元，较年初增加39.69亿元，增长7.74%；各项存款余额401.89亿元，较年初增加52.04亿元，增长14.87%；各项贷款余额280.89亿元，较年初增加29.32亿元；不良贷款余额44326万元，较年初增加13349万元，不良率1.41%，上升0.18个百分点；实现净利润5.12亿元；缴纳各种税金4.88亿元；资本充足率11.87%，较上年下降2.78个百分点；资产利润率1.46%；成本收入比29.38%；拨备覆盖率204.36%。2014年，在英国《银行家》杂志评选出的全球前1000家银行中，莱商银行排名第672位。

## （二）强化战略管理，积极推进战略实施

2014年，董事会及战略发展委员会着力督促推进各项战略规划落地实施，公司转型发展效果显著。一是认清形势，把握方向。面对外部严峻宏观经济形势，监管政策日趋严格，利率市场化进程的推进，互联网金融的发展，公司从中小银行坚持自己市场定位出发，报告期内制定了《莱商银行业务转型创新发展规划》，提出了转型的思路。二是推进机构网点建设。报告期内完成了日照分行的筹建，5家支行开业，3家支行批筹，1家支行报筹；14家小微和社区支行，3家支行开业，8家支行报筹，3家支行完成选址工作。三是加强投资管理，推进村镇银行建设。截止2014年12月31日，村镇银行资产总额97.98亿元，存款余额88.78亿元，贷款余额48.15亿元，共实现利润2.53亿元，其中归属公司的利润为2582万元。投资德州银行占比15.4%，报告期内实现分红1746.36万元，投资齐商银行占比1.27%，报告期内10送2，分红40万元。

## （三）致力提升服务水平，支持地方经济发展

2014年，董事会认真贯彻国家宏观调控政策，通过推进网点转型，持续提升服务质量，释放经营活力，完善提高服务地方经济、中小企业和城乡居民的能力。一是提升科技支撑，加快产品业务创新。随着科技支撑业务能力的提升，手机银行客户端、微信银行相继上线，即时贷、循环贷等产品也相继推出，丰富了公司产品及业务种类。二是加强网点规范化服务建设，打造营销型、服务型网点。董事会要求经营层引导分支行改变营销模式，走出去营销，深入社区营销，通过各种宣传、活动，为社区居民讲解金融知识，宣传业务产品，帮助社区居民理财，提升服务居

民的能力。三是下沉业务重心，支持小微企业发展。公司按照董事会提出“小银行服务小企业”的理念，将发展落脚在小微金融服务上，引导分支行大力发展小微企业客户，同时创新产品，优化业务流程，建立适合公司特点的小微信贷考察审批模式，在风险可控的前提下尽量简化业务手续、提高业务效率。四是继续发展主办行制度和信用贷款业务。2014年，公司已累计向信用贷款客户发放信用贷款28.19亿元，签发承兑汇票18.25亿元，无一笔贷款形成不良。人民银行济南分行将公司主办行制度和信用贷款业务作为典型经验在全省范围内进行了通报。

## （四）健全制度，完善内控，有效控制各类风险

董事会始终坚持依法合规经营，加强制度建设，高度警惕利润与风险的错配，坚持盈利的可持续性和风险的可控性相结合，不断提升风险管理水平，为莱商银行的发展保驾护航。一是加强内控管理，提高合规意识。公司积极配合监管，报告期内根据监管部门的现场检查意见书和监管提示，经营层成立整改领导小组，负责整改工作的组织领导及协调推进，确保整改工作落到实处。此外启动合规手册编写，完善内控合规管理制度体系，明确每个关键岗位操作规范及其违规处罚标准，进一步提高员工对制度敬畏感和合规自律性。二是启动全面风险管理系统建设，推动全面风险管理评估，全面梳理风险管理现状，初步形成了符合监管要求及公司实际的全面风险管理体系。三是强化重点领域的风险管控。董事会持续关注政府融资平台、民间借贷、钢贸、纺织、“两高一剩”等领域的信贷风险，督促高管层健全立体化、全方位的重点领域风险监测机制，持续开展定期风险排查，有效避免了风险积聚。

## （五）推进资本管理，保障业务稳健运营

董事会严格按照资本办法的规定和相关监管要求，切实履行资本管理职责，全面提高资本抵御风险和支持保障经营管理的能力。一是完善资本管理规划，制定了《莱商银行资本管理规划（2014-2016）》，保证资本充足指标持续达标。二是加强资本管理的评估。公司大力倡导资本集约型发展方式，在绩效考核中将资本约束作为考核内容适当提高占比，推进经营管理方式转变满足资本管理的要求。三是积极探索资本补充渠道。根据公司机构拓展和业务发展增速要求，积极寻求拓宽资本补充渠道，董事会专门在会议上对进入新三板并发行优先股进行了讨论，向同业学习进入新三板的经验，适时推进。

## （六）维护利益相关者权益，不断践行社会责任

2014年，公司继续总结丰富企业文化，通过组织各类活动和加大宣传力度使莱商文化深入人心，同时积极践行企业公民责任，回报社会。一是积极维护股东权益。2014年，在2013年完成资本公积金转增股本后，现金分红每股0.05元（含税），分享公司的经营发展成果的同时，维护良好的投资者关系，提升公司的外部形象。二是以人为本，为员工搭建发展平台。董事会提出以组织架构调整为契机，梳理公司的人力资源管理体系，年内完成了总行20个部室、34个中心的人员

配备，并完成了职责的交接，将部分人事管理职能下放到分行、管辖行，重新梳理了管理职级和薪酬体系，按照银监会的要求实施了绩效薪酬的延期支付。三是继续参加公益事业。2014年公司继续参加春蕾计划和慈心一日捐，继续冠名山东女排，参加青年志愿者活动，参与省市慈善事业，支持青年创业，积极践行社会责任，社会知名度和公信力得到提升。

## 三、董事会日常工作情况

报告期内公司董事会共召开了4次会议，具体情况如下：

（一）2014年2月11日，召开第三届董事会第十一次会议，审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会2013年度工作报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年度经营工作报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年度审计报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年度财务报告及利润分配方案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司发展转型规划》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2014年机构发展计划的报告》、《莱芜钢铁集团有限公司与鲁银投资集团股份有限公司资产置换的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年度内部审计工作报告》、《关于对（莱商银行股份有限公司2013年度报告）进行信息披露的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年度内部人和股东关联交易执行情况的报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年度风险管理报告》、《关于审议<信息科技2014年度重大项目及总体预算>的议案》、《关于审议<莱商银行信息科技三年发展规划（2014-2016）>的议案》、《关于审议<莱商银行信息科技外包战略>的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司关于召开2013年度股东大会的议案》。

（二）2014年2月8日，以通讯方式召开第三届董事会第十二次会议，审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会对董事2013年度履职评价报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会对高级管理人员2013年度履职评价报告》。

（三）2014年5月22日，召开第三届董事会第十三次会议，审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司2014年1-3月经营工作报告》、《关于更换会计师事务所的议案》、《关于提取2013年度长期激励约束基金的议案》、《关于修改公司章程的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2014年1-3月内部人和股东关联交易执行情况的报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2014年1-3月风险管理报告》、《关于对外股权投资管理情况的报告》、《关于购买济宁分行营业楼的议案》、《关于购买菏泽分行营业楼的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司三年资本规划（2014-2016）》。

（四）2014年9月15日，以通讯方式召开第三届董事会第十四次会议，审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司2014年1-6月经营工作报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司公司治理自评估报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2014年1-6月内部人和股东关联交易执行情况的报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2014年1-6月风险管理报告》、《关于成立理财业务部的议案》、《关于成立资产保全部的议案》、《关于召开莱商银行股份有限公司2014年第一次临时股东大会的议案》。

## 四、2015年工作计划

## (一) 工作思路：

面对当前经济形势持续下行，监管政策继续趋紧的新常态，继续坚持积极稳妥的发展战略，

按照速度服从质量的原则，合理调整资产负债结构，使同业业务、理财业务、国际业务等亮点业务和传统业务匹配增长；坚守中小企业市场定位，增加中小企业客户数量和粘度；以网点转型为契机带动业务转型，以客户为中心，下沉服务，发展和创新个人业务品种，增加核心负债依存度；科学制定人才发展战略与规划，推动实现人力资源资本化；加强全面风险管理，继续推行全员控风险；拓宽资本补充渠道，实现资本有效支撑业务及机构发展，增强核心竞争力。

## (二) 工作目标：

1. 确保资本充足率达到监管要求：

2. 年存款增长15%，存贷比控制在75%以内；

3. 实现拨备前利润（税前）11.5亿元，资本利润率保持在11%以上，成本收入比控制在35%以内，中间业务收入占比8%以上；

4. 不良贷款率控制在5%以内，贷款损失准备充足率在150%以上，拨贷比达到2.5%以上；

5. 监管指标符合二类行标准；

6. 保证信息披露数字真实准确；

7. 实现安全营运全年无道德案件和重大责任案件发生。

## (三) 工作重点

1. 优化公司治理运行机制。一是继续修订完善公司治理制度，按照《商业银行公司治理指引》及省银监局批复的《公司章程》，完善公司治理，保障董事会、股东大会规范履职和顺畅运作。二是完成董事会换届工作，并调整各专门委员会，充实各委员会成员，发挥其决策咨询功能。三是探索和丰富董事履职方式。通过增加调研，使董事了解公司发展现状，从公司治理层面为创新和转型发展提出切合实际的意见和建议。四是推进公司治理授权体系规范运行，明确有权签字人，并在授权后加强对执行情况的监督检查。

2. 建立战略管理体系，推动规划落地实施。按照监管要求，明确战略管理部门，并制定战略管理办法推进顶层制度设计，并在总战略体系下，建立人力资源、科技信息、公司业务、零售业务等子战略，探索互联网金融和直销银行模式，借助城商行联盟和战略投资者的平台，推进科技与金融的紧密结合，创新经营模式、盈利模式和服务模式，增强公司的经营实力。

3. 强化全面风险管理。在安永提出的全面风险管理评估报告的基础上，使全面风险管理系统上线运行，使风险管理覆盖业务发展的全流程，持续提升全面风险管理的有效性。持续关注重点领域的风险管控，着力提升风险缓释能力，并对出现的风险严格问责。坚持合规经营，推进“全员控风险”的风控文化，完善案防管理体系，及时消除各类案件风险隐患。

4. 改进和提升资本管理能力。按照资本办法和监管要求以及《莱商银行股份有限公司资本管理规划》的要求，建立内部资本充足评估体系，并适时开展压力测试。深入分析未来资本充足的情况，做好进入新三板挂牌、发行优先股、增资扩股、发行二级资本债等资本补充工作，保障业

务可持续发展。继续完善经济资本管理，主动调整资产结构，推动公司实现低资本消耗下的集约化经营。

5. 提高综合服务能力。不断深化服务理念，完善分层面、多功能、多渠道服务客户的方法，加强客户细分，增强科技支撑力、产品竞争力和营销服务能力，对于优质中小客户，从利率、额度、期限上给予照顾。同时继续推动网点转型，创新社区和小微支行发展的模式，以小微业务促进管理和服务提升，形成完善的管理、营销、服务、风控、盈利模式的转型，大力发展中间业务，同时着力研究传统业务的利润增长点，对于亮点业务，继续发挥优势，做好同业、国际、理财等业务，做好发行20亿元涉农金融债工作，提高公司的综合实力和盈利能力。

6. 加强人力资源管理。通过制定人力资源管理规划，保证人力资源管理活动于公司的战略方向目标一致，推动人力资源实现人力资本的过渡，细化岗位职责与配置，使每一位员工优化配置在最合适的位置上，以最低成本实现人力资源效用的最优发挥。结合岗位需要，为员工制定发展计划，提高员工的忧患意识，开发员工的个人潜能，为员工发展自我提供广阔的空间，形成公司创新发展的动力。

## 五、利润分配方案

按照10%的比例计提法定盈余公积，按2%的比例计提任意盈余公积，每股按0.10元（含税）进行分配，按规定提取长期激励计划基金。上述分配方案执行后，结余未分配利润作为企业积累转作下年使用。

# Chapter VII Report of the Board of Directors

## I. Review of main operation and management in 2014

## (i) Steady operation and scientific development and further upgrading of comprehensive strength

The total assets reached RMB 55.251 billion, increasing by RMB 3.969 billion as compared to that at the beginning of the year, representing a growth rate of 7.74%; the balance of various deposits was RMB 40.189 billion, increasing by RMB 5.204 billion as compared to that at the beginning of the year, representing a growth rate of 14.87%; the balance of various loans was RMB 28.089 billion, increasing by RMB 2.932 billion as compared to that at the beginning of the year; the balance of non-performing loans was RMB 443.26 million, increasing by RMB 133.49 million as compared to that at the beginning of the year, and the non-performing rate was 1.41%, increasing by 0.18 percentage point; the net profit realized was RMB 512 million; various taxes paid reached RMB 488 million; the capital adequacy ratio was 11.87%, decreasing by 2.78 percentage points as compared with that of the previous year; the return on assets was 1.46%; the cost income ratio was 29.38%; and the provision coverage was 204.36%. In 2014, the Bank ranked No. 672 among the top 1,000 banks in the world issued by British magazine The Banker.

## (ii) Enhance strategic management and actively promote strategy implementation

Board of Shareholders and Strategic Development Committee made great efforts to supervise and urge the implementation of various strategic planning and the Company harvested a significant result of transformation and development in 2014. Firstly, understand the situation and grasp the direction. Facing the external severe macro-economic situation, the gradually strict regulatory policies, the smooth interest rate marketization process and internet finance development, the Company adhered to its market positioning as a small and medium-sized bank and formulated Business Transformation and Innovative Development Planning of Laishang Bank during the reporting period, putting forward its thought about transformation. Secondly, speed up the organization network construction. During the reporting period, the Company finished the preparation and construction of Rizhao Branch; 5 sub-branches started business; 3 obtained the construction approval and 1 declared construction; of 14 small banks and community sub-branches, 3 started business, 8 declared construction and 3 finished site selection. Thirdly, enhance investment management and speed up rural bank construction. By December 31, 2014, total assets of rural banks reached RMB 9.798 billion; the balance of deposits was RMB 8.878 billion; the balance of loans was RMB 4.815 billion; the total profits realized reached RMB 253 million, among which RMB 25.82 million belonged to the Company; the investment made in Dezhou Bank accounted for 15.4% and dividends achieved were RMB 17.4636 million during the reporting period; the investment made in Qishang Bank accounted for 1.27%; during the reporting period, dividends were RMB 400 thousand through the method of buying 10 sending 2.

(iii) Dedicate to improving service levels and supporting local economic development

In 2014, Board of Directors earnestly implemented the macro-regulation policies, sped up network transformation and continuously improved the service quality, released management vitality, perfected and improved its ability to serve local economics, small and medium-sized enterprises and urban and rural residents. Firstly, improve science and technology support and speed up products and business innovation. Owing to the improvement in science- and-technology-supported business abilities, the come-out of mobile banking client-side, We-chat banking, the instant loans and revolving credit, our bank's products and business lines became abundant. Secondly, enhance outlet standardized service construction and create the outlets featured with marketing and service. Under the requirement of Board of Directors, the management levels lead all branches and sub-branches to change their marketing models and adopt "going out to marketing" approach, which meant marketing deep into communities. By means of various advertisement and activities, we taught financial knowledge and introduced business products to community residents, helping them manage money matters and improving their abilities. Thirdly, sink business core and support the development of small and micro businesses. On the basis of the concepts that "small banks serve small businesses" proposed by Board of Directors, the Company oriented its development in the service of the small finance. It guided all branches and sub-branches vigorously to develop small business clients while creating new products, optimizing business process. As a result, it established a small and micro credit and loan examination and approval mode fit for the Company's features, and simplified business procedures and improved business efficiency on the premise of controllable risks. Fourthly, further promote leading bank system and credit loan business. In 2014, our bank has provided RMB 2.819 billion in total to the clients of credit loan, signed and issued acceptance bills of RMB 1.825 billion, without non-performing loans. The People's Bank of China Jinan Branch took our practice of leading bank system and credit loan business as a model and notified it throughout the province.

## (iv) Perfect the system and the internal control and effectively control all kinds of risks

Always adhering to compliance business, enhancing institutional construction, being cautious about the mismatch of profits and risks and combining the sustainability of earnings with the controllable risks, the Board of Directors improved risk management level and escorted Laishang Bank's safety operations. Firstly, enhance internal control management and improve compliance awareness. The Company co-operated in the supervision and regulation actively. During the reporting period, according to the on-spot inspection opinions by supervision departments and their supervision and regulation tips, the Company established a leading group among management levels for rectification. The group was responsible for organizing, leading, promoting steadily the rectification work and ensuring its implementation. Besides, the Company started to write the compliance handbook and perfected internal control system, clarifying the operation specification and punishment standards for infringement of each key post, further improving employees' sense of owe to the system and their compliance of self-discipline. Secondly, launch comprehensive risk management construction and promote comprehensive risk management assessment, showing an overall risk management status and initial shaping a comprehensive risk management system that met the supervision requirements and real conditions of the Company. Thirdly, strengthen risk

management and control in important fields. Board of Directors paid close attention to government financing vehicles, private lending, steel trading, textile, “two high and one remaining” and the credit risks in other fields. They supervised and urged the top management levels to establish three-dimensional and all-round important field risks supervision mechanism, checked risks regularly and effectively avoided risk accumulation.

#### (v) Advance capital management and guarantee stable business operation

Board of Directors implemented capital management responsibility in real earnest strictly according to specifications of capital methods and relevant supervision requirements, comprehensively increasing capital's capacity of resisting risk and supporting and safeguarding operation management. Firstly, improve capital management planning, and enact Capital Management Planning (2014-2016) of Laishang Bank to ensure that index of capital adequacy can continuously reach the standard. Secondly, enhance evaluation of capital management. The Company vigorously advocated capital intensive development mode, and put capital constraints as testing content in performance assessment and appropriately increased proportion, promoted transformation of operation and management mode to satisfy the demand of capital management. Thirdly, actively explore capital complement channels. According to the Company's requirements for speeding up the development of institutions and business, it sought ways to broaden capital complement channels. Board of Directors made special discussions about the new OTCBB and launched preferred stock, learning the experience of entering the new OTCBB from its peers and seeking an appropriate time to promote it.

## (vi) Maintain interests of stakeholders and continuously fulfill social responsibility

In 2014, the Company continued to summarize and enrich enterprise culture, and made Laishang culture enjoy popular support by organizing various activities and intensifying propaganda, meanwhile, positively practiced enterprise and civic responsibility to pay back the society. First of all, positively maintain shareholders' equity. In 2014, cash bonus was 0.05 yuan per share (including tax) after the completion of turning capital reserve into equity in 2013. While sharing management and development fruits of the Company, sound investor relations were maintained to improve external image of the Company. Secondly, put people first, and establish development platform for employees. Board of Directors proposed that collate the Company's human resources management system with the opportunity of organizational structure adjustment. Personnel allocation of 20 Department Offices and 34 Centers in Head Office was completed within the year as well as handover of responsibilities; lowered part of personnel management functions to branches, administrative branches; collated the management job grades and salary system again, and implemented deferred payments of performance salary in accordance with requirements of CBRC. Thirdly, continue to participate in public welfare establishments. In 2014, the Company continued to be engaged in Spring Buds Program and one-day donation out of kindness, sponsored for naming Shandong Women's volleyball team, participated in young volunteer activities, province and city philanthropy, supported youth startup and positively fulfilled social responsibility, which increased the Company's social popularity and credibility.

## III. Daily work of the Board of Directors

Four meetings of the Board of Directors have been held during the reporting period. Details are as follows:

(i) The 11th Meeting of the 3rd Board of Directors was convened on February 11, 2014 and the following proposals were reviewed and approved, including: 2013 Annual Report of Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2013 Annual Management Work Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2013 Annual Audit Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2013 Annual Financial Report and Profit Distribution Plan of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Planning of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.'s Development Transformation, Report of Institutional Development Plan of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. in 2014, Asset Exchange Proposal between Laiwu Steel Group Co., Ltd. and Luyin Investment Group Co., Ltd., 2013 Internal Audit Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Information Disclosure about <2013 Annual Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.>, Executive Conditions Report of Related Transaction between Insider and Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. in 2013, 2013 Annual Risk Management Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Reviewing <2014 IT Major Projects and Overall Budgets>, Proposal on Reviewing <Three-year Development Planning of Laishang Bank's IT (2014-2016)>, Proposal on Reviewing <Outsourcing Strategy of Laishang Bank's IT>, and Proposal on Holding 2013 Annual General Meeting of Stockholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.

(ii) The 12th Meeting of the 3rd Board of Directors was convened on February 8, 2014 and the following proposals were reviewed and approved, including: 2013 Annual Evaluation Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. Made by Board of Supervisors for the Resumption of Directors, and 2013 Annual Evaluation Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. Made by Board of Supervisors for the Resumption of Senior Management.

(iii) The 13th Meeting of the 3rd Board of Directors was convened on May 22, 2014 and the following proposals were reviewed and approved, including: Management Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. from January to March, 2014, Proposal on Replacing Accounting Firm, Proposal on Withdrawing Long-term Incentive and Constraint Funds in 2013, Proposal on Modifying Articles of Association, Executive Conditions Report of Related Transaction between Insider and Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. from January to March, 2014, Risk Management Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. from January to March, 2014, Report of Foreign Equity Investment Management Conditions, Proposal on Purchasing the Business Building of Jining Branch, and Proposal on Purchasing the Business Building of Heze Branch, Three-year (2014-2016) Capital Planning of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.

(iv) The 14th Meeting of the 3rd Board of Directors was convened on September 15, 2014 and the following proposals were reviewed and approved, including: Management Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. from January to June, 2014, Company Governance Self-assessment Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Executive Conditions Report of Related Transaction between Insider and

Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. from January to June, 2014, Risk Management Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. from January to June, 2014, Proposal on Establishing Finance Department, Proposal on Establishing Asset Preservation Department, and Proposal on Holding the First Extraordinary Shareholders Meeting of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. in 2014.

## IV. 2015 Work Plan of Board of Directors

##### (i) Thinking of work

Facing the continued descending of current economic situation and the new normality of tightening supervisory policy, the Board of Directors shall continuously stick to active and sound development strategies; reasonably adjust assets and liabilities structure to make distinctive businesses such as the interbank business, financial services, and international business increase matching with traditional business pursuant to principle that quality is superior to speed; stick to market positioning of middle and small-sized enterprise, and increase quantity and durability of middle and small-sized enterprise customers; drive business transformation with turning point of branch transformation, and focus on customers and sink services to develop and innovate individual business varieties and increase core delt dependency; scientifically formulate development strategy and planning of talented person, and push forward capitalization of human resources; strengthen comprehensive risk management, and further put into force the overall risk control; broaden capital complement channel, and achieve capital's effective support of business and institutional development, which makes for the increase of core competitiveness.

##### (ii) Work objectives:

1. Guarantee that capital adequacy ratio can satisfy supervision requirement;

2. Annual deposit shall increase by 15%, and loan-to-deposit ratio shall be controlled under 75%;

3. Achieve profit before provision (before taxes) of RMB 1.15 billion, and maintain return of equity beyond 11%. Control cost-income ratio under 35%, and income of intermediate business shall account for more than 8%;

4. Control non-performing loan ratio under 5%, and maintain loan loss reserves adequacy beyond 150%, moreover, provision-loan ratio shall be more than 2.5%;

5. Supervision index shall conform to type II industry standard;

6. Guarantee that figures of information disclosure are true and accurate;

7. Achieve safe operation, without moral case or weighty responsibility case in the whole year.

#### (iii) Work focus

1. Optimize operating mechanism of company governance. First of all, continue to revise and improve the corporate governance system; revise corporate governance system in accordance with Guidance on Corporate Governance of Commercial Bank and Articles of Association replied and

approved by Provincial Banking Bureau to ensure normative resumption of Board and the General Meeting of stockholders and smooth operation. Secondly, complete the transition work of Board of Directors, and adjust each special committee, moreover, enrich member structure of each committee, and give play to its policy-making consulting function. The third point is to explore and enrich resumption way of directors. Make directors understand the corporate development status through investigation, and put forward opinions and suggestions for improvement of corporate governance, reinforcement of innovation and transformation development. Fourthly, promote standardized operation of authorization system in corporate governance, and make sure the authorized signatories, finally, enhance supervision and inspection of executive conditions after authorization.

2. Establish strategic management system, and promote the implementation of planning. On the basis of supervision requirement, determine Strategic Management Department, and formulate strategic management method to promote top system design, and establish sub-strategies such as human resources, scientific and technical information, corporate business, retail business and so on under overall strategic system. Explore internet finance and direct selling bank pattern. Boost close integration of technology and finance, and innovate business model, profit model and service model by virtue of the platform of city commercial banks' alliance and strategic investors to enhance business capacity of the Company.

3. Strengthen comprehensive risk management. On the basis of overall risk management assessment report proposed by Ernst & Young, make overall risk management system be online and operated, make risk management cover the whole process of business development, and continuously improve effectiveness of overall risk management. Continuously focus on risk control in key fields, and put efforts into improving risk mitigation capacity, and strict accountability shall be conducted for emerging risk. Uphold operation in accordance with regulations, and promote risk control culture of "overall risk control". Improve case prevention management system, and promptly eliminate risk and hidden danger of all kinds of cases.

4. Improve and promote capital management capacity. Establish internal capital adequacy evaluation system, and carry out pressure test in due time pursuant to capital methods, supervision requirements as well as requirements in Capital Management Planning of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. Deeply analyze capital adequacy in the future, and be well prepared for capital complement work such as to be listed in new OTCBB, issue of preferred stock, increase in capital and share, issuance of the secondary capital debt and so on, ensuring sustainable development of business. Further improve economic capital management, and initially adjust asset structure to drive the Company to realize intensive management with low capital consumption.

5. Improve integrated service capacity. Constantly deepen service concept, and improve ways and means of serving customers in different levels, multifunction and multiple channels. Strengthen customer segmentation, and enhance science and technology holding power, product competitiveness and marketing service capacity. As for high-quality middle and small-sized customers, privileges in aspects of interest rate, amount limit and deadline shall be given to them. Branch transformation

shall be promoted at the same time, and innovate development mode of community branches and small and micro branches. Take advantage of small and micro business to promote improvement of management and service, and form thorough transformation of management, marketing, service, risk control, and profit model. Strive to develop intermediate business, meanwhile, focus on the study of profits growth points of traditional business. The advantages of distinctive business shall be continuously given play to. Soundly conduct the interbank business, international business, financial business, and etc, as well as the issue of RMB 2 billion of financial bonds involved in agriculture. Enhance the corporate comprehensive strength and profitability.

6. Strengthen human resources management. Ensure that human resources management activities are consistent with strategic direction of the Company. Push forward human resources to achieve transition of human capital; refine position responsibilities and allocation to make each employee be optimized in the most suitable place, and achieve optimum development of human resources utility in the minimum cost. Enact development plan for employees combining with post needs to increase employees' awareness of unexpected development, develop individual potential and provide broad space for employees' self-development, which is helpful to form impetus of the Company's innovation and development.

## V. Profit distribution plan

Legal surplus reserve has been allotted at a rate of 10%, discretionary surplus reserve has been allotted at a rate of 2%. Dividends have been allotted at RMB 0.10 per share (before tax) and long-term incentive plan fund is extracted subject to regulation. After the implementation of the above distribution plan, the retained profit will be taken as enterprise fund, to be used in the next year.

（二）2014年2月28日，以通讯方式召开第三届监事会第十一次会议，审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对监事2013年度履职评价报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对董事2013年度履职评价报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对高级管理人员2013年度履职评价报告》。

## 二、监事会就有关事项发表的独立意见

## （一）公司依法经营情况

报告期内，公司坚持依法合规经营，不断完善内部控制制度，决策程序符合法律、法规及公司章程的有关规定。董事会、高级管理层成员认真履行职责。未发现其履行职务时有违反法律、法规、《公司章程》或损害公司利益的行为。

## （二）财务报告真实性

2014年度财务报告已由中兴华会计师事务所根据《企业会计制度》和《企业会计准则》有关规定进行审计，并出具标准无保留意见审计报告。财务报告真实、公允地反映了公司财务状况和经营成果。

## （三）关联交易情况

公司的关联交易主要是股东贷款。监事会认为，报告期内，公司在处理关联交易时，严格遵守有关规定，履行关联交易操作程序，交易过程体现了公平合理原则，关联交易符合商业原则，未发现损害公司及股东利益的行为。

## （四）股东大会决议执行情况

监事会对董事会提交股东大会审议的各项报告和提案进行了查阅和调研，没有异议。监事会对股东大会决议的执行情况进行了监督，认为董事会能够按照公司治理要求，认真执行了股东大会的有关决议。

### II. Independent opinion on relevant issues from the Board of Supervisors

#### (i) Lawful operation of the Company

During the reporting period, the Company has stuck to operation in accordance with laws and regulations, and kept improving the internal control system. The decision-making procedure met the related provisions of laws, regulations and the Articles of Association. The directors and the senior management were able to conscientiously implement their duties, and no director or senior management has been discovered to have breached any laws or regulations or Articles of Association or harmed the interest of the Company when they execute their functions.

#### (ii) Authenticity of financial reports

Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP has carefully audited the 2014 Annual Financial Report in accordance with Accounting System for Enterprises and Accounting Standard for Enterprises and issued standard unqualified audit report. The financial report has truthfully and fairly reflected the Company's financial position and operation results.

##### (iii) The related transactions

The related transactions of the Company are mainly shareholders' loans. The Board of Supervisors thinks that during the reporting period when dealing with related transactions, the Company has strictly observed related regulations and performed the operating procedure of related transactions. Transaction processes have reflected the principle of fairness and justifiableness. The related transactions meet the commercial principles and no action compromising the interests of the Company or shareholders has been discovered.

## (iv) Implementation of the resolutions approved in the general meeting of shareholders

The Board of Supervisors has reviewed and investigated all reports and proposals submitted by the Board of Directors to the general meeting of shareholders for deliberation, without any objection. The Board of Supervisors has supervised over the implementation of the resolutions approved in the general meeting of shareholders, and thinks that the Board of Directors of the Company is able to conscientiously implement the related resolutions of the general meeting of shareholders according to the requirements of corporate governance.

## 一、公司持有其他公司股权和参股金融企业股权情况

2004年9月，向德州市商业银行（2010年6月更名为德州银行）投资3,000万元，投资比例为6%，并于2009年12月新增投资24,000万元，投资比例为15.4%；2005年向淄博市商业银行（2009年3月更名为齐商银行）投资2,000万元，投资比例为1.27%；2008年7月，向山东省城市商业银行合作联盟有限公司投资1000万元，占注册资本的7.14%，于2009年增加投资1000万元，占注册资本的7.41%，2011年12月增加投资2000万元，占注册资本的8.99%。2009年9月，向河南方城凤裕村镇银行有限责任公司投资510万元，占注册资本的51%，于2011年7月，追加投资510万元，占注册资本的51%；2010年10月，向东营莱商村镇银行股份有限公司投资3300万元，占注册资本的51%，2011年11月，追加投资5370万元，占注册资本的28.9%；2013年3月，向天津武清村镇银行股份有限公司投资9800万元，占注册资本的49%；2014年1月，向五台莱商村镇银行股份有限公司投资800万元，占注册资本的40%。

## 三、重大关联交易事项

2014年度无重大收购、出售资产、吸收合并事项。

报告期末，公司单一客户贷款集中度7.72%，单一集团客户授信集中度14.26%，授信集中度97.13%；公司单一客户关联度为8.07%，集团客户关联度为13.43%，全部关联度为44.09%，均符合监管要求。

公司关联交易公平合理，贷款审批程序按照公司现有的信贷业务审批和管理制度执行，贷款利率参照公司利率定价模型进行测算，并遵循市场化原则执行，不存在损害股东权益及公司利益的情况。公司关联方泰丰纺织集团有限公司经营状况和财务状况持续下行，存在履约风险。公司其余关联方经营状况和财务状况良好，能够履行与公司达成的各项协议，报告期内公司关联交易未产生不良资产。

Chapter IX Significant Events

## I. Equity of other companies held by the Company and equity participating in other financial enterprises

The Company invested RMB 30 million to Dezhou Commercial Bank (its name was changed into Dezhou Bank in June, 2010) in September, 2004, and the investment proportion accounted for 6%; the investment of RMB 240 million was newly added in December, 2009, and the investment proportion accounted for 15.4%. The Company invested RMB 20 million to Zibo Commercial Bank (its name was changed into Qishang Bank in March, 2009), and the investment proportion accounted for 1.27%. The Company invested RMB 10 million to Shandong City Commercial Banks Alliance in July, 2008, which accounted for 7.14% of registered capital; and RMB 10 million was added in 2009, which accounted for 7.41%; another investment of RMB 20 million was added in December, 2011, which accounted for 8.99% of registered capital. The Company also invested RMB 5.1 million to Henan Fangcheng Fengyu County Bank Co., Ltd. in September, 2009, which accounted

for 51% of registered capital; additional investment of RMB 5.1 million was added in July, 2011, which accounted for 51% of registered capital. The Company invested RMB 33 million to Dongying Laishang County Bank Co., Ltd. in October, 2010, which accounted for 51% of registered capital; additional investment of RMB 53.7 million was added in November, 2011, which accounted for 28.9% of registered capital. The Company invested RMB 98 million to Tianjin Wuqing County Bank Co., Ltd. in March, 2013, which accounted for 49% of registered capital. The Company invested RMB 8 million to Wutai Laishang Rural Bank Co., Ltd. in January 2014, which accounted for 40% of registered capital.

### II. Acquisition and sell for assets and absorption and merger There was no significant acquisition or sell for assets or absorption and merger in 2014.

At the end of the reporting period, the single client loan concentration was 7.72%, the credit concentration ratio of a single group client was 14.26%, and the credit concentration ratio was 97.13%; the single client correlation was 8.07%, the group client correlation was 13.43%, and the generalized correlation ratio was 44.09%, all of which met regulatory requirements.

The Company’s related transaction was fair and reasonable, and procedures for examination and approval of loan were implemented according to the Company’s current credit business approval and management system. Loan interest rate was calculated referring to pricing model of the Company’s interest rate and implemented by following marketization principle and conditions that damage shareholders’ equity and corporate interest didn’t exist. The operating conditions and financial conditions of Taifeng Textile Group Co., Ltd, the related party, continuously descend, and contractual risk existed. Apart from that, operating conditions and financial conditions of other related parties of the Company were in order, and those related parties can perform agreements concluded with the Company. Non-performing asset has not been generated in related transactions during reporting period.

During the reporting period, there is no major fund raising activity of the Company.

In 2014, the Company employed Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP (special general partnership) to audit 2014 annual financial statement of the Company.

During the reporting period, the Company, any director, supervisor or senior management has not been subject to any punishment of the related regulatory authorities or judicial department.

None.
